{"lang_code":"en","pos":"phrase","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"This town is very unattractive to tourists, since there's nothing to see here.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used other than figuratively or idiomatically: see nothing, to, see, here."]},{"examples":[{"text":"After the press conference about the financial scandal, the spokesperson reassured the public that there was nothing to see here.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The event or situation is unremarkable or unimportant, and people should not pay undue attention to it."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The politician's vague response to corruption allegations was essentially a nothing-to-see-here moment.","type":"example"},{"text":"A factory discharged waste into the river, but officials insisted it posed no danger.
